Seated as claims BK22A-01..10: the plains of moab — the book's last camp; balak saw the conquest; moab feared — the sojourn-verb; moab loathed — the manna's verb; the ox licking the field; the elders of midian — the thread to the war; pethor by the river — the translation names aram and the euphrates; the locusts' clause for israel; three curse-roots, twenty tokens; whom you bless is blessed; divinations in their hand; god came to balaam — the three gentiles' verse; the lord refuses — pharaoh's verb; the second embassy; i will surely honor you — promised here, revoked at 24:11; his house full of silver and gold — the first of two; god came at night — only the word i speak, that do: the formula and its restrictor; the akedah's morning in balaam's; god's anger — the first of three; the angel as an adversary — the satan-word's two torah seats; the angel of the lord's ten; the she-ass saw the angel three times; the sword drawn, plene; the path of the vineyards; balaam's foot; she crouched — judah's verb; the staff; the lord opened the mouth of the she-ass; these three feet — the times-word, and the plene three the parser cannot read; you have made sport of me — egypt's verb; would there were a sword — he dies by it; from your youth; the lord uncovered balaam's eyes — his own title afterward; the sword defective; he bowed to his face — joseph's clause; i came out as an adversary; the way contrary; i have sinned — pharaoh's confession; nothing but the word, that speak; balak meets him at the city of moab on the arnon — the border 21:13 drew; kiriath-huzoth; the sacrifice; bamoth-baal — the well-song's station; the edge of the people. 'And Moab FEARED before the people' (22:3) — the token is Abraham's 'and he SOJOURNED in Gerar' (Gen 20:1), one skin for dwelling and dread; 'and Moab LOATHED the children of Israel' — the verb of 21:5 'our soul LOATHES the light bread', Rebekah's 'I loathe my life' (Gen 27:46), the land's 'I loathed them' (Lev 20:23): Israel loathed the manna, Moab loathes Israel (computed on the points); 'the assembly will LICK UP all our surroundings as the ox licks the green of the field' (22:4) — the lick-verb's one Torah seat (Elijah's fire 'licked up the water', 1 Kgs 18:38); 'Moab said to THE ELDERS OF MIDIAN' (one seat) — Midian enters at 22:4 and 22:7, vanishes through the oracles, returns with Cozbi (25:6-18): the ink's thread to chapter 31 (computed). Onkelos: 'loathed' → DISTRESS; 'lick up' → DESTROY (cut). 		—Onkelos Num 22:3, Onkelos Num 22:4witnessed
